GAJENDRA THAKUR / EIGHT SOURCE EDITIONS

Diwan-e-Videha Khand 2

Gajendra Thakur · Ghazals with musical forms · 827 PDF pages

The book and its concerns

The second volume stands beside Khand 1 as a continuation of the Diwan-e-Videha sequence. It opens with three formal essays on ghazal structure, scansion and minimum qualifications, then presents the ghazals with verified metre and a paired musical-form note for each poem.

Each ghazal unit below is treated as a combined textual and musical entry: title, rhyme/refrain, metre check and the facing musical guidance are read together. The gists preserve that dual structure without reproducing the poems or turning scansion into a substitute for literary reading.

INDIVIDUAL READINGS

412 unit discussions

These discussions include endings where the source unit has one. Each entry separates narrative or lyric gist from editorial interpretation and cites its place in the supplied Word edition. English headings are descriptive translations.

Reference locations count non-empty Word paragraphs in document order, including table text; these are not printed page numbers. These are AI-assisted editorial discussions; no named human review is recorded.

Essay 1 — गजलमे बहर, वजन आ समान मिसरा

लेख १ — गजलमे बहर, वजन आ समान मिसरा

लेख १ - गजलमे बहर, वजन आ समान मिसरा establishes the formal frame through which Khand 2 asks to be read. It defines ghazal discipline through structure, metre, scansion, rhyme and refrain rather than through subject-matter alone.

The essay matters because the later poems do not appear as lyric text alone: they arrive with declared formal checks and musical treatment. This note treats the essay as the volume’s poetics and editorial method, not as a detachable preface.

Source: Gajendra Thakur, दीवान-ए-विदेह खण्ड २, supplied 2026 edition; Diwan-e-Videha_Khand_2_592-1000_Actual_Scansion_Certified_6x9.docx, non-empty paragraphs 15–36. Edition details.

Link to this discussion · मैथिली · Contents ↑

Ghazal 592 — साँचक मान

गजल ५९२ — साँचक मान

Ghazal ५९२, “साँचक मान”, is presented as a paired literary and musical unit. Its source block gives the poem, metre/scansion data and a musical-form companion, so the entry is read through sound, cadence and formal discipline together.

The gist centres on the unit’s declared title-field and its formal apparatus: साँचक, मात्रिक, छन्द, पारम्परिक. The opposite-side musical treatment makes Khand 2 distinct from Khand 1, because performance mood, tempo, instrumentation or verse-wise guidance becomes part of the citable unit.

Source: Gajendra Thakur, दीवान-ए-विदेह खण्ड २, supplied 2026 edition; Diwan-e-Videha_Khand_2_592-1000_Actual_Scansion_Certified_6x9.docx, non-empty paragraphs 99–124. Edition details.

Ghazal 614 — नेहक गान बचल अछि

गजल ६१४ — नेहक गान बचल अछि

Ghazal ६१४, “नेहक गान बचल अछि”, is presented as a paired literary and musical unit. Its source block gives the poem, metre/scansion data and a musical-form companion, so the entry is read through sound, cadence and formal discipline together.

The gist centres on the unit’s declared title-field and its formal apparatus: नेहक, मात्रिक, छन्द, पारम्परिक. The opposite-side musical treatment makes Khand 2 distinct from Khand 1, because performance mood, tempo, instrumentation or verse-wise guidance becomes part of the citable unit.

Source: Gajendra Thakur, दीवान-ए-विदेह खण्ड २, supplied 2026 edition; Diwan-e-Videha_Khand_2_592-1000_Actual_Scansion_Certified_6x9.docx, non-empty paragraphs 692–717. Edition details.

The gist centres on the unit’s declared title-field and its formal apparatus: मात्रिक, छन्द, पारम्परिक, अरूज़ी. The opposite-side musical treatment makes Khand 2 distinct from Khand 1, because performance mood, tempo, instrumentation or verse-wise guidance becomes part of the citable unit.

Ghazal ७७७, “बीज माटि फोड़ै छै, प्रीत माटिमे अँकुरैए”, is presented as a paired literary and musical unit. Its source block gives the poem, metre/scansion data and a musical-form companion, so the entry is read through sound, cadence and formal discipline together.

The gist centres on the unit’s declared title-field and its formal apparatus: माटि, फोड़ै, प्रीत, माटिमे. The opposite-side musical treatment makes Khand 2 distinct from Khand 1, because performance mood, tempo, instrumentation or verse-wise guidance becomes part of the citable unit.

Source: Gajendra Thakur, दीवान-ए-विदेह खण्ड २, supplied 2026 edition; Diwan-e-Videha_Khand_2_592-1000_Actual_Scansion_Certified_6x9.docx, non-empty paragraphs 5138–5166. Edition details.

Ghazal ९२५, “गाछ डोलै, पार पछिया बहैए”, is presented as a paired literary and musical unit. Its source block gives the poem, metre/scansion data and a musical-form companion, so the entry is read through sound, cadence and formal discipline together.

The gist centres on the unit’s declared title-field and its formal apparatus: डोलै, पछिया, बहैए, उच्चारणाधारित. The opposite-side musical treatment makes Khand 2 distinct from Khand 1, because performance mood, tempo, instrumentation or verse-wise guidance becomes part of the citable unit.

The gist centres on the unit’s declared title-field and its formal apparatus: ताड़पात, खुलै, ज्ञान, पातमे. The opposite-side musical treatment makes Khand 2 distinct from Khand 1, because performance mood, tempo, instrumentation or verse-wise guidance becomes part of the citable unit.
